FOREWORD BY THE EXECUTIVE BOARD




Carsten Salewski, Dr. Martin Heuser, Dirk Schwingel (f. l. t. r.)




     WE MAKE TECHNOLOGY SAFE,
     RELIABLE AND SUSTAINABLE.


Viscom has been developing, manufacturing and selling high-              Our mission is to achieve the highest quality in our
quality inspection systems for 40 years now. Our product                 customers‘ production processes to ensure a flawless finished
portfolio covers the entire range of optical inspection and              product for the consumer. Viscom is one of the world‘s
X-ray inspection, especially for electronic assemblies. Viscom           leading suppliers of high-end inspection systems. Renowned
inspection systems are used wherever the requirements for                customers rely on our experience and Viscom‘s innovative
inspection accuracy and inspection speed are particularly high.          strength.

                                                It is now 40 years since Viscom SE was founded. A lot has
                                                happened in that time: a two-man company has become a
                                                high-tech company with an international presence. Dr. Heuser,
                                                how do you look back?


                                                Dr. Martin Heuser: I look back with pride on the life‘s work of
                                                Mr. Pape and myself. Over the past 40 years, we have travelled
                                                an impressive journey together. From humble beginnings to
                                                an established company that is highly regarded in the industry,
Dr. Martin Heuser                               we have overcome many challenges and achieved significant
Chief Development and Production Officer        milestones.


                                                Each year has been characterised by innovation, team
                                                spirit and the tireless efforts of our employees. We have not
                                                only positioned Viscom as a technological market leader,
                                                but have also built relationships based on trust and cooperation.
                                                We have also created a corporate culture characterised by
                                                respect and appreciation as well as the Viscom „we“ feeling.


                                                With a broad product range that is adapted to the highest
                                                technological requirements and the consistent integration of
                                                modern trends such as artificial intelligence and Industry 5.0,
                                                Viscom stands out on the market with its high-precision fault
                                                detection. Our products are characterised by their durability,
                                                upgradeability and adaptability, which creates long-term
                                                customer relationships and cross-selling potential. Viscom‘s
                                                strong brand, high profile in the industry and focus on new
                                                market potential ensure a stable market position and growth
                                                opportunities in new sectors.


                                                Unfortunately, 2024 was not a year to celebrate, which
                                                Mr. Pape and I would have liked to have been different. We
                                                also had to take personnel measures to ensure the continued
                                                existence of our company in order to reposition Viscom
                                                efficiently and profitably. I am very confident that with our
                                                still strong team and the adjustments within the company, we
                                                have put Viscom back on a competitive footing.

                                                                     25
Mr. Schwingel, as Chief Financial Officer, you faced major
challenges in the 2024 financial year. What specific measures
have you taken to get Viscom‘s cost base back on track?


Dirk Schwingel: The past financial year was a very difficult year
for our company. Unfortunately, we had to make serious cuts
in order to be able to cover a profitable core business with
the existing structures. We took measures across the Group at
the beginning of the year to master the economic situation
while conserving liquidity. In addition, works agreements                   Dirk Schwingel
were concluded at the Hanover site in close consultation with               Chief Financial Officer
the works council, allowing short-time working from March
to November 2024. As part of a voluntary programme during
short-time working, we offered employees at the Hanover site
the opportunity to leave the company at short notice. This offer
was taken up by 52 employees. Further redundancies were
announced in December 2024. Most of the employees affected
accepted the offer to join a transition (outplacement) company.
We will initially realise future growth by adaptive staffing –
through temporary work or outsourcing.


You will not distribute a dividend for the 2024 financial year
due to the accumulated loss. Can the shareholders of Viscom SE
expect a dividend again in subsequent years?


Dirk Schwingel: We are working every day to make Viscom
profitable and fit for the future again. In the 2024 financial year,
we also had to make unpleasant decisions for Viscom to achieve
this. As a result of the measures taken last year, we expect to
return to moderate corporate profits, which our shareholders
can expect. We intend to continue to adhere to our well-known
and communicated dividend policy and distribute at least 50 %
of the reported positive consolidated net profit for the period
to our shareholders.

REPORT ON RISKS AND OPPORTUNITIES

Expected opportunities                                                    If better progress is made in these research and development
                                                                          activities than currently expected, this could lead to more
Electronics are pushing forward ever further into all areas of            new and improved products being launched on the market or
life. Electronic assemblies are becoming smaller and smaller,             new products being available earlier than planned. This could
while at the same time they are expected to fulfil more                   have a positive impact on revenue and earnings and result in
functions. This technological diversification requires high-class         Viscom exceeding its medium-term outlook.
inspection solutions: solutions that ensure product quality but
also guarantee the long-term stability of increasingly complex            Risk management strategy, process and
processes. The requirements for Viscom inspection systems                 organisation
are becoming increasingly specific. This dynamic market
environment is constantly opening up new opportunities for                As a globally active Group, Viscom is exposed to a wide variety
the Viscom Group. Systematically recognising and using these              of risks. For this reason, a comprehensive risk management
opportunities is a key factor in Viscom's sustainable growth.             system has been set up to identify and analyse potential events
                                                                          – which could endanger the Group – at an early stage and take
Viscom regularly evaluates market and competitive analyses                appropriate countermeasures. The risk management system
and undertakes the corresponding alignment of the product                 comprises a large number of control mechanisms and forms a
portfolio. Specific market opportunities are derived from these           key element of the corporate decision-making process.

Viscom SE's strategy for identifying, assessing and improving                                           practices within the company, Viscom SE has focused on
the material effects of resource use and the circular economy                                           improving resource use and introducing the principles of the
is currently in the concept phase and is to be adopted in 2025                                          circular economy. Viscom SE's goals and measures include:
and will cover the following areas in the future:
                                                                                                        The expansion of the cycle-orientated product design:
• Determination: Resource inventory and life cycle analysis                                             Viscom SE develops its products so that they are durable,
• Assessment: Materiality assessment and risk assessment                                                dismantlable, repairable and recyclable. This helps to extend
• Improvement: Research and development and employee                                                    the service life of the products.
     engagement
                                                                                                        The minimisation of primary raw materials: The aim is to
These three areas contribute to an improvement in resource                                              reduce the consumption of primary raw materials. Viscom SE
use and strengthen the circular economy by highlighting the                                             achieves this by efficiently utilising and reusing materials in its
potential for increased resource use.                                                                   production processes.

resources.                                                                                              minimises its environmental impact by reducing and recycling

            these activities.
